## Engine evidence standard

Actual automated duels use public `@n1xx1/ocgcore-wasm` with Omega Lua helpers. Native Omega executable was unavailable. These are not native Omega certifications.

Syntax and registration alone are not passes. Require legal/illegal activation, actual outcomes, chain interactions, restrictions/count limits/reset evidence proportional to each effect. State unsupported APIs and fixture modifications explicitly. Never turn a test-engine gap into a production compatibility simplification.

Known public-core gaps: `Card.IsCanOverlay`, `Duel.CheckXyzMaterial`, and some native Synchro material APIs. Certain Fusion fixtures supply neutral mandatory-material bindings only when no such restriction exists. See each harness/report before relying on it.

Public core suppresses EVENT_TO_GRAVE on banishment-to-GY movement even in an in-memory negative control that removes REASON_RETURN; it cannot prove that distinction. Production uses REASON_EFFECT+REASON_RETURN to match official Omega c11110218.

Important fixture lesson: directly initializing a normal Pendulum in Extra Deck silently placed it in Deck in this core. Earlier apparent Speeaker face-up Extra recruitment passes were superseded. Current tests create genuine face-up Extra cards through field-to-GY movement (engine redirects Pendulums), and assert selected source location. Face-up Extra cards may have FACEUP_DEFENSE position, not FACEUP_ATTACK.

## Useful commands/tests

Run in workspace. Use `node --no-warnings` (sqlite warning suppression) and `luac -p` for syntax.

```
python scripts/stage_remote_omega.py
python scripts/test_september_banlist.py
node --no-warnings scripts/fresh_underroot_afterroot_swap.cjs
node --no-warnings scripts/fresh_underroot_afterroot_levels.cjs
node --no-warnings scripts/fresh_underroot_overroot_set.cjs --afterroot
node --no-warnings scripts/fresh_underroot_terror_blossom_battle.cjs --afterroot
node --no-warnings scripts/fresh_symphonic_speeaker_hand.cjs
node --no-warnings scripts/fresh_symphonic_speeaker_hand.cjs --pendulum
node --no-warnings scripts/fresh_underroot_terror_search.cjs --speeaker
node --no-warnings scripts/fresh_underroot_terror_search.cjs --speeaker --recruit-hand
node --no-warnings scripts/fresh_underroot_terror_search.cjs --speeaker --recruit-extra
node --no-warnings scripts/fresh_underroot_terror_search.cjs --speeaker --prior=faceup-pendulum
node --no-warnings scripts/fresh_underroot_terror_search.cjs --speeaker --later=fusion
node --no-warnings scripts/fresh_symphonic_mettronomo_deck.cjs
node --no-warnings scripts/fresh_symphonic_mettronomo_hand.cjs

```

Prior/later variants also accept wind-synchro, pendulum-fusion, faceup-pendulum. Do not combine later fixtures with prior/source variants; harness guard disallows it. Negative controls intentionally fail and must not be mistaken for production failures. Inspect JSON engine qualifiers.
